Opened run 20261106-012054-coordination-variants-c1376c7: candidates mirror, replicas 1 2 3, 5 repetitions, phases up to 1800 s
Machine: the official one, 24 hardware threads, 8 P cores and 16 E cores
Factors: coordination mode (hazelcast activemq activemq-persistent polling orchestrator) and replicas of crawler and indexer together (1 2 3), 5 repetitions each, interleaved: the 15 configurations of repetition 1, then repetition 2, so drift spreads across every mode
H0: download_ms does not differ between any pair of modes, nor indexing_ms between any pair without polling (differs false in pairs.csv), so only the coordination changed
H0 in polling: a batch claims each book as it polls it, so the wait for the batch to fill counts in indexing_ms instead of index_queue_wait; their sum compares through time_to_publication
polling H1: throughput within the CI of hazelcast, since the work per book dominates
polling H2: MongoDB CPU higher than in hazelcast at every N: one sorted findOneAndUpdate per claim, plus the idle polls
polling H3: publication visibility median about 2.5 s
activemq H1: throughput and time to publication within the CI of hazelcast at every N
activemq-persistent H2: throughput within the CI of activemq, with more broker CPU
orchestrator H1: throughput within the CI of hazelcast at N = 1 and 2; at N = 3 it may fall below, because one thread sends one POST at a time
orchestrator H2: time to publication up to two ticks (400 ms) above hazelcast
orchestrator H3: publication visibility under 300 ms
